Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the stochastic_analysis framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 766. The resulting operator achieves bound O(250/n^5).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on stochastic_analysis structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 766. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 250. QED.
